| `capture/grid-facts.ts`                      | Which of a grid's columns are as wide as their widest item, and whether each is as wide as its items' least width, as the page reads them                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `capture/flex-room.ts`                       | The room a flex line leaves past its items once its auto margins give theirs back, with its items packed at its start, and whether forcing `nowrap` moves a wrapping line's items, as the page reads them                                                                                                                                    |
| `capture/element-content.ts`                 | Each element's child nodes in order where its `text` and `textIndex` lose some of its text, as the page reads them                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `capture/no-box.ts`                          | Which elements generate no box and add nothing to their parent's flow but their children's boxes, as the page reads them                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `capture/clear-fill.ts`                      | Whether a background color the page read paints nothing, by its alpha through `@elmeragroup/color`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `capture/read-page.ts`, `run-capture.ts`     | The capture: the DevTools reads of each cell, the forced-winner check, and the set's capture                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `capture/winners.ts`                         | Each element's winning declarations from the rules Chromium matched, and the class each rule selects through                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `capture/capture-cascade.ts`                 | The cascade each set's mapping reads from its capture: the elements around the root, the fill behind it, and every box's computed style                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `capture/manifest.ts`, `capture/captures.ts` | The manifest's fields and the definition the mapper walks for it, and the kept captures                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `capture/oracle.ts`                          | Reads `oracles/coverage.json`, the ledger of the rows the kit keeps unmapped, unbound or unshown, with a reason for each                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `capture/coinciding-around.ts`               | How many elements around a root lie exactly behind it, which decides whose background the root shows                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `capture/pseudo-elements.ts`                 | What a pseudo-element's cascaded declarations paint, its border shorthands split, and the cascade of one the mapping draws                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `oracles/coverage.json`                      | The coverage ledger the oracle test reads. `oracles/plan.json` is the frozen record for the owner's real-file Apply                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `oracles/keys.json`                          | The frozen keys `test/frozen-keys.test.ts` holds the build to, which `figma:keys` appends to                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `capture/perturbation.ts`                    | The perturbation probe: each box's widths in three runs, the sizing they imply, and the contradictions with the built set                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

The tests run the whole command line against `test/in-memory-figma.ts`. That fake file applies batches with the REST API's documented rules, stores numbers as 32-bit floats and resolves aliases the way Figma renders them. Where the documentation is silent, the fake does what a real Enterprise file did on 2026-10-16. It replaces code syntax on an update, it checks for an alias cycle after each value a batch sets, and it refuses any cycle, even one that closes only across modes. It deletes a collection with its modes and variables and keeps an alias into it until a later value in the batch replaces it, as a real Enterprise file did on 2026-10-19 when one batch deleted `Fuse themes` and then rewrote the `Fuse tokens` aliases into it. It also applies the scope rules on the REST API's [variable types page](https://developers.figma.com/docs/rest-api/variables-types/). It rejects a scope the page does not list for the variable's type, such as `GAP` on a color, `ALL_SCOPES` beside another scope, and `ALL_FILLS` beside another fill scope. It follows a real Enterprise file where that file departed from the page on 2026-10-16. It keeps `FONT_STYLE` on a number variable and stores `FONT_WEIGHT` there as `FONT_STYLE`. It stores a description with `&`, `<`, `>`, `"` and `'` HTML-escaped, as a real Enterprise file did on 2026-10-19. Tests can also make it answer with a status, drop a connection, or hold values and types newer than the sync.
